\displaystyle=\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{pppp}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{pppq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 3},\nabla_{p}F]-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{ppq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{ppqq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{pq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]]

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla_{pqq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{p}F,\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{pqqq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 3}]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{q}(H_{0})\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 3}]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{p}F,\nabla_{ppp}{F}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qqq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}(H_{0})[(\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G)^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{qqqq}(H_{0})[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]+0-0+0-0+0-0-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 3}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}G[\nabla_{p}F,\nabla_{ppp}{F}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]]-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}G[(\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G)^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}G[(\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G)^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}G[\nabla_{p}F,\nabla_{ppp}{F}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]],

(189)

\displaystyle\Omega_{3}(H_{1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{q}(H_{1})\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]+\nabla_{pq}(H_{1})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]-\nabla_{qq}(H_{1})[\nabla_{p}F,\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{ppp}(H_{1})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 3}]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{ppq}(H_{1})[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2},\nabla_{p}F]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{pqq}(H_{1})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{qqq}(H_{1})[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 3}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G+\frac{1}{2}[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]-\frac{1}{4}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{ppp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]-\frac{1}{2}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{ppp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3}{4}[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}],

(190)

\displaystyle\Omega_{2}(H_{2})

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{q}(H_{2})\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{pp}(H_{2})\left[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}\right]-\nabla_{pq}(H_{2})[\nabla_{q}G,\nabla_{p}F]+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{qq}(H_{2})\left[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{6}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G-\frac{1}{12}[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{6}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{ppp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F-\frac{1}{6}[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G-\frac{1}{6}[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G+\frac{1}{24}\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}],

(191)

\displaystyle\Omega_{1}(H_{3})

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{p}(H_{3})+\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{q}(H_{3})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G+\frac{1}{12}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{ppp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{12}[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{ppp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F+\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F-\frac{1}{12}\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}].

(192)

We now sum (
189
), (
190
), (
191
) and (
192
):

\displaystyle\Omega_{4}(H_{0})+\Omega_{3}(H_{1})+\Omega_{2}(H_{2})+\Omega_{1}(H_{3})=

\displaystyle=\left(-\frac{1}{8}+\frac{1}{12}+\frac{1}{24}\right)\nabla_{pppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 4}]+\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{12}\right)\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{ppp}F[\nabla_{q}G^{\otimes 2}]

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{12}+\frac{1}{12}\right)\nabla_{q}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G+\left(\frac{1}{12}-\frac{1}{12}\right)\nabla_{p}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{qq}G\nabla_{p}F

\displaystyle\quad+\left(-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{4}-\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}\right)[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 2}]\nabla_{qqq}G\nabla_{pp}F\nabla_{q}G+\left(\frac{1}{24}-\frac{1}{12}+\frac{1}{24}\right)\nabla_{qqqq}G[\nabla_{p}F^{\otimes 4}]

\displaystyle=0.

(193)

Thus, the case where
N=3
is verified. To get an upper bound on the
N=2
case, assuming that
F
and
G
are
L
-smooth of orders
1,\dots,4
, (
174
) implies

\displaystyle\left|\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(2\right)}\left(p_{n+1},q_{n+1}\right)-\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(2\right)}\left(p_{n},q_{n}\right)\right|

,

F.2
Verification up to
N=10
via symbolic calculation of the coefficients

Unlike in Appendix
F.1
, the following approach only works for
d=1
dimensions since SymPy
[
53
]
does not currently have support for higher-order derivative tensors. To compute the successive terms of the modified Hamiltonian in SymPy, we implement the recursive form of the BCH formula as stated in
[
20
]
and Section 2.15 in
[
81
]
, and proved as Lemma 2.15.3 in
[
81
]
. When applied to the setting of symplectic Euler and Alternating Mirror Descent, we recover the following for any
N\in\mathbb{N}
:

\displaystyle H_{0}

\displaystyle=F+G,

(198a)

\displaystyle NH_{N}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\{H_{N-1},G-F\right\}+\sum_{p=1}^{\lfloor(N-1)/2\rfloor}{\frac{B_{2p}}{\left(2p\right)!}\left(\mathrm{ad}_{\widetilde{H}}^{2p}\left(F+G\right)\right)_{N}},

(198b)

where

\displaystyle\left(\mathrm{ad}_{\widetilde{H}}^{2p}\left(F+G\right)\right)_{N}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1}+\dots+k_{2p}=N-1\\
k_{1}\geq 1,\dots,k_{2p}\geq 1\end{subarray}}{\left\{\left(F+G\right)H_{k_{2p}-1}\cdots H_{k_{1}-1}\right\}}

(199)

denotes the projection of
\mathrm{ad}_{\widetilde{H}}^{2p}\left(F+G\right)
onto the homogeneous subspace
\mathcal{L}\left(F,G\right)_{N+1}
consisting of degree
N+1
elements from
\mathcal{L}\left(F,G\right)
, as defined in (
50
).

In SymPy
[
53
]
, we use the recursive formula above to compute the order-by-order corrections to the modified Hamiltonian
H_{k}
. We have uploaded our code onto a repository,
[
42
]
. These are then used to compute the values of
C_{j,k}=\Omega_{j}(H_{k})
for each fixed value of
j+k-1=N
to check Conjecture
1
at each order in
N
. These are also used to compute the remainders for fixed values of
j+k=N+1
.

We essentially use the formula (
171
) to compute the action of
\Omega_{j}
on each
H_{k}
as needed. However, we do this by expanding both exponentials as power series in
\eta
up to sufficiently high order, from which we can compute the derivatives symbolically with respect to
\eta
and then evaluate the result at
\eta=0
.

The necessary computations to check Conjecture
1
when
N=11
can in principle be run in Python.
However, due to the explosion in the number of terms, the program ran into memory and rounding issues when trying to cancel out all of the fractions in the resultant expressions and show that the terms vanished.
We leave an investigation of a more efficient program for the verification of the above conjecture as future work.

F.3
Concluding the proof

In the prior subsections of Appendix
F
, we confirmed (
175
) for orders
N\in\{0,1,2,3\}
by direct computation for arbitrry dimension
d
;
we also confirmed the cases
N\in\{0,1,\dots,10\}
by computer for dimension
d=1
. However, (
175
) only bounds the difference in
\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{(N)}
after one iteration while Conjecture
1
gives the same for multiple iterations. To derive a bound after
k
iterations, we apply (
175
) recursively and then the triangle inequality as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(N\right)}\left(p_{k},q_{k}\right)-\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(N\right)}\left(p_{0},q_{0}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}{\left|\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(N\right)}\left(p_{j+1},q_{j+1}\right)-\widetilde{H}_{\eta}^{\left(N\right)}\left(p_{j},q_{j}\right)\right|}

\displaystyle\leq k\Phi\left(N\right)L^{N+3}\eta^{N+2},

where in the first inequality above we use triangle inequality. This concludes the proof.

Characterization of

from Conjecture

has the following known values for

It is not immediately obvious as to why
\Phi
should be bounded. However, up to
N=10
, we note that
\Phi\left(N\right)=2\frac{\gamma(N)}{\sigma\left(N+2\right)}
, where
\gamma:\mathbb{N}\rightarrow\mathbb{N}
returns an unknown sequence of positive integers

and
\sigma:\mathbb{N}\rightarrow\mathbb{N}
returns the Hirzebruch numbers, which can be defined as follows
[
59
]
:

\displaystyle\sigma\left(n\right)\coloneqq\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}2\leq p\leq n+1\\
p\textrm{ prime}\end{subarray}}{p^{\lfloor\frac{n}{p-1}\rfloor}}\quad\forall n\in\mathbb{N}.

As proven in
[
10
, Corollary 9]
,
\sigma\left(N\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(N^{N}\right)
for
N
large. Furthermore,
\gamma\left(N\right)\leq\frac{2}{3}\left(N+2\right)^{N+2}
is true up to

and
only
saturates at
N=1
. Thus, assuming that
\mathcal{O}\left(\gamma\left(N\right)\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(N^{N}\right)
remains true for
N>10
, it remains plausible (although hitherto unproven) that
\mathcal{O}\left(\Phi\left(N\right)\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(1\right)
for large
N
. Hence, we suspect that
\Phi
is monotonic increasing and bounded as claimed in Conjecture
1
.
